It corrected an initially numerical reading into a relation between created forms.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_5e1a83b5e585a7b611b8","text":"The later orphan and poor transformed `الشفع والوتر` from numerical polarity into a social scene: helperhood, advocacy, and the injury of being left single all became active.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000802/B002","root_001621/B002","root_001621/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_6d0c6695a1c6dad890b8","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"89:3:1\",\"root_000802\",[\"B002\"]],[\"89:3:2\",\"root_001621\",[\"B002\",\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Çift, koruyucu ve yardımcı bir ilişkinin kurulmasını; tek ise hakkından yoksun bırakılarak yalnız kalan kişiyi görünür kılar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000802/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_8092e37b2cdcfcc6f76c","text":"1. **Relational operations, not merely two number classes.** **Evidence/cue:** `ش ف ع B001` is the act of joining a thing to its like, whereas `و ت ر B001` is the unpaired single. At the far edge of the window, 89:8 denies a likeness to Iram through `م ث ل B001`. **Mechanism:** *shafʿ* supplies counterparting and *watr* supplies the remainder when counterparting fails; Iram's lack of a like is the packet's concrete relational test. I infer the application of the numerical pair to created forms. **Reading change:** the oath names the operations of finding a counterpart and standing unmatched, with evenness and oddness as their results.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000802/B001","root_001621/B001","root_001621/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_eea792632befb1591755","text":"1. **Pairing and singling as social accounting.** Lexical evidence: `ش ف ع B001` joining a thing to its like; `و ت ر B001` odd single rather than a pair; `و ت ر B003` diminution that takes away a due. Structural cue: later contrasts come in paired diagnoses and claims: honor/disgrace, orphan/poor, eating/loving, punishment/binding; 89:25-26 end with `أَحَدٌ` as exhaustive singularity (`ء ح د B002`). Assigned role: the even/odd pair is an accounting grammar for what is joined to its due and what is left isolated or diminished. Abductive move: I infer that later legal-social losses activate `و ت ر` as deprivation, not merely oddness. Reading change: the ayah reads as a compact figure of rights being paired or stripped away.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000802/B002","root_001621/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ff6d9c917acba6f2e160","text":"2. **Intercession/helperhood versus unhelped isolation.** Lexical evidence: `ش ف ع B002` joining as advocate or helper; `و ت ر B002` blood-feud injury calling for redress; later `ح ض ض B001` urging toward feeding and `ي ت م B001` orphaned offspring cut off from protection. Structural cue: the social section condemns failure to honor and failure to urge. Assigned role: `الشفع` supplies the missing advocate/helper relation; `الوتر` marks the one left wronged and unredressed. Abductive move: I infer that the orphan and poor retrospectively specify who has been left “odd.” Reading change: the ayah becomes an oath by the structure of social accompaniment and abandonment.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
